```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog


def compute_c5_vec(h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full') * dx


def compute_c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(compute_c5_vec(h, n)))


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)

    for _ in range(30):
        s = float(h.sum())
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = float(room.sum())
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h = h + (diff / tot) * room
        else:
            tot = s
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h = h + (diff / tot) * h

    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    s = float(h.sum())
    diff = target - s

    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        if diff > 0:
            order = np.argsort(1.0 - h)
            for ii in order:
                if s >= target:
                    break
                add = min(1.0 - h[ii], target - s)
                if add > 0.0:
                    h[ii] = h[ii] + add
                    s = s + add
        else:
            order = np.argsort(h)
            for ii in order:
                if s <= target:
                    break
                sub = min(h[ii], s - target)
                if sub > 0.0:
                    h[ii] = h[ii] - sub
                    s = s - sub

    return h


def resample_by_mass(h, m):
    n = len(h)
    if m <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0)
    if m == n:
        return project_feasible(h, n)

    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    xp = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n + 1)
    cp = np.empty(n + 1, dtype=float)
    cp[0] = 0.0
    cp[1:] = np.cumsum(h) * dx
    cp = np.clip(cp, 0.0, 1.0)
    cp[-1] = 1.0

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, m + 1)
    cnew = np.interp(x, xp, cp, left=0.0, right=1.0)
    mass = np.diff(cnew)
    mass = np.clip(mass, 0.0, 1.0)
    hnew = mass * (float(m) / 2.0)
    return project_feasible(hnew, m)


def rand_feasible(n):
    return project_feasible(np.random.rand(n), n)


def rand_binary(n):
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    idx = np.random.permutation(n)
    k = int(np.floor(target))
    if k > n:
        k = n
    if k > 0:
        h[idx[:k]] = 1.0
    rem = target - float(k)
    if rem > 1e-12 and k < n:
        h[idx[k]] = rem
    return h


def default_h(n=500):
    return rand_binary(n)


def make_binary(h, n):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    idx = np.argsort(-h)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    k = int(np.floor(target))
    if k > n:
        k = n
    if k > 0:
        b[idx[:k]] = 1.0
    rem = target - float(k)
    if rem > 1e-12 and k < n:
        b[idx[k]] = rem
    return b


def single_shift_grad(h, n, idx):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    k = int(idx) - (n - 1)
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    if k >= 0:
        nv = n - k
        if nv > 0:
            grad[0:nv] += (-h[k:n]) * dx
            grad[k:n] += (1.0 - h[0:nv]) * dx
    else:
        kk = -k
        nv = n - kk
        if nv > 0:
            grad[kk:n] += (-h[0:nv]) * dx
            grad[0:nv] += (1.0 - h[kk:n]) * dx

    return grad


def weighted_grad_direct(w, h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    for m in range(len(w)):
        wm = float(w[m])
        if abs(wm) < 1e-12:
            continue

        k = m - (n - 1)
        if k >= 0:
            nv = n - k
            if nv > 0:
                grad[0:nv] += wm * (-h[k:n])
                grad[k:n] += wm * (1.0 - h[0:nv])
        else:
            kk = -k
            nv = n - kk
            if nv > 0:
                grad[kk:n] += wm * (-h[0:nv])
                grad[0:nv] += wm * (1.0 - h[kk:n])

    return grad * dx


def smooth_obj(x, n, alpha, mu):
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    c = compute_c5_vec(x, n)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    sh = alpha * (c - m)
    mx = float(np.max(sh))
    logsum = float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(sh - mx))))
    val = m + (mx + logsum) / float(alpha)
    s = float(np.sum(x)) - 0.5 * float(n)
    val += float(mu) * s * s
    return float(val)


def smooth_grad(x, n, alpha, mu):
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    c = compute_c5_vec(x, n)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    sh = alpha * (c - m)
    mx = float(np.max(sh))
    w = np.exp(sh - mx)
    tot = float(w.sum())
    if tot <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w = w / tot

    grad = weighted_grad_direct(w, x, n)
    s = float(np.sum(x)) - 0.5 * float(n)
    grad += (2.0 * float(mu) * s) * np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    return grad


def slqp_obj(x, n, alpha):
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    c = compute_c5_vec(x, n)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    sh = alpha * (c - m)
    mx = float(np.max(sh))
    return float(m + (mx + float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(sh - mx))))) / float(alpha))


def slqp_eq(x, n):
    return float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * float(n))


def slqp_eq_jac(x, n):
    return np.ones(n, dtype=float)


def lbfgs_optimize(h, n, deadline, configs=None, maxiter_override=None):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h, n)
    if time.time() > deadline:
        return best_h, best_c

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n

    if configs is None:
        if n <= 64:
            configs = [(20.0, 10.0), (100.0, 100.0), (500.0, 1000.0), (2000.0, 10000.0)]
        elif n <= 256:
            configs = [(100.0, 10.0), (500.0, 100.0), (2000.0, 1000.0), (8000.0, 10000.0)]
        else:
            configs = [(500.0, 10.0), (2000.0, 100.0), (8000.0, 1000.0)]

    for alpha, mu in configs:
        if time.time() > deadline - 2.0:
            break

        if n <= 64:
            maxiter = 1200
        elif n <= 256:
            maxiter = 800
        else:
            maxiter = 500

        if maxiter_override is not None:
            maxiter = int(maxiter_override)

        maxfun = max(50, maxiter * 20)

        try:
            res = minimize(
                smooth_obj,
                h,
                jac=smooth_grad,
                args=(n, float(alpha), float(mu)),
                method='L-BFGS-B',
                bounds=bounds,
                options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol': 1e-16, 'gtol': 1e-12, 'maxfun': maxfun}
            )
            h_new = project_feasible(res.x, n)
            c_new = compute_c5(h_new, n)
            if c_new < best_c - 1e-15:
                best_c = c_new
                best_h = h_new.copy()
                h = h_new
        except Exception:
            pass

    return best_h, best_c


def slqp_polish(h, n, deadline, alpha=100000.0, maxiter=500):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_c = compute_c5(h, n)
    if time.time() > deadline - 5.0:
        return h, best_c

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': slqp_eq, 'jac': slqp_eq_jac, 'args': (n,)}]

    try:
        res = minimize(
            slqp_obj,
            h,
            args=(n, float(alpha)),
            method='SLSQP',
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons,
            options={'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'ftol': 1e-15}
        )
        h_new = project_feasible(res.x, n)
        c_new = compute_c5(h_new, n)
        if c_new < best_c:
            return h_new, c_new
    except Exception:
        pass

    return h, best_c


def sqp_optimize(h, n, deadline, max_iter=None, rho_init=0.05, max_active=None):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h, n)
    if time.time() > deadline:
        return best_h, best_c

    if max_iter is None:
        if n <= 80:
            max_iter = 250
        elif n <= 250:
            max_iter = 140
        elif n <= 600:
            max_iter = 90
        else:
            max_iter = 50

    if max_active is None:
        if n <= 80:
            max_active = 2 * n - 1
        elif n <= 250:
            max_active = 120
        elif n <= 600:
            max_active = 70
        else:
            max_active = 40

    rho = float(rho_init)
    no_improve = 0

    for _ in range(int(max_iter)):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break

        c = compute_c5_vec(h, n)
        maxv = float(np.max(c))
        tol = max(1e-10, 1e-4 * maxv)

        active = np.where(c >= maxv - tol)[0]
        if active.size == 0:
            active = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])

        if active.size > int(max_active):
            active = active[np.argsort(-c[active])[:int(max_active)]]

        A_ub = np.zeros((len(active), n + 1), dtype=float)
        b_ub = np.zeros(len(active), dtype=float)

        row = 0
        for idx in active:
            g = single_shift_grad(h, n, int(idx))
            A_ub[row, 0:n] = g
            A_ub[row, n] = -1.0
            b_ub[row] = -float(c[int(idx)])
            row += 1

        A_eq = np.zeros((1, n + 1), dtype=float)
        A_eq[0, 0:n] = 1.0
        b_eq = np.zeros(1, dtype=float)

        c_obj = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=float)
        c_obj[n] = 1.0

        bounds = []
        for i in range(n):
            lo = max(-rho, -float(h[i]))
            up = min(rho, 1.0 - float(h[i]))
            if lo > up:
                lo = up = 0.0
            bounds.append((lo, up))
        bounds.append((0.0, None))

        delta = None
        try:
            res = linprog(
                c_obj,
                A_ub=A_ub,
                b_ub=b_ub,
                A_eq=A_eq,
                b_eq=b_eq,
                bounds=bounds,
                method='highs'
            )
            if res.success:
                delta = res.x[0:n]
        except Exception:
            delta = None

        if delta is None:
            rho *= 0.5
            no_improve += 1
            if rho < 1e-8 or no_improve > 20:
                break
            continue

        if float(np.max(np.abs(delta))) < 1e-13:
            break

        improved = False
        for factor in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125):
            h_try = project_feasible(h + factor * delta, n)
            c_try = compute_c5(h_try, n)
            if c_try < best_c - 1e-15:
                best_c = c_try
                best_h = h_try.copy()
                h = h_try
                improved = True
                break

        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
            rho = min(0.15, rho * 1.25)
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            rho *= 0.5
            if rho < 1e-9 or no_improve > 25:
                break

    return best_h, best_c


def deriv_lag_vector(h, n, p):
    L = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
    start = n - 1 - int(p)
    L[start:start + n] -= h
    L[int(p):int(p) + n] += 1.0 - h[::-1]
    return L


def pair_candidate_max(base, diff, mp1, mp2, dx, t):
    t = float(t)
    if t == 0.0:
        return float(np.max(base))
    vals = base + dx * t * diff
    vals[int(mp1)] += dx * t * t
    if int(mp2) != int(mp1):
        vals[int(mp2)] += dx * t * t
    return float(np.max(vals))


def pair_line_search(c_base, h, n, i, j, Li, Lj):
    i = int(i)
    j = int(j)

    tmin = -float(h[i])
    tmax = min(1.0 - float(h[i]), float(h[j]))

    if tmax - tmin < 1e-7:
        return 0.0, float(np.max(c_base))

    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    d = abs(i - j)
    mp1 = n - 1 + d
    mp2 = n - 1 - d

    diff = Li - Lj

    best_t = 0.0
    best_c = float(np.max(c_base))

    lo0 = tmin
    hi0 = tmax
    step = (hi0 - lo0) / 40.0

    ts = np.linspace(lo0, hi0, 41)
    for t in ts:
        val = pair_candidate_max(c_base, diff, mp1, mp2, dx, t)
        if val < best_c:
            best_c = val
            best_t = float(t)

    for _ in range(3):
        lo = max(tmin, best_t - 2.0 * step)
        hi = min(tmax, best_t + 2.0 * step)
        if hi - lo <= 1e-9:
            break
        ts = np.linspace(lo, hi, 81)
        for t in ts:
            val = pair_candidate_max(c_base, diff, mp1, mp2, dx, t)
            if val < best_c:
                best_c = val
                best_t = float(t)
        step = (hi - lo) / 80.0

    return best_t, best_c


def pair_descent(h, n, deadline, max_iter=80, max_pairs=200, active_window=0.002):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = compute_c5_vec(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = float(np.max(c))
    no_improve = 0

    for _ in range(int(max_iter)):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break

        maxv = float(np.max(c))
        window = max(1e-9, float(active_window) * maxv)

        act = np.where(c >= maxv - window)[0]
        if act.size == 0:
            act = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])

        if act.size > 60:
            act = act[np.argsort(-c[act])[:60]]

        w = np.zeros(len(c), dtype=float)
        w[act] = 1.0
        w /= float(act.size)

        g = weighted_grad_direct(w, h, n)

        interior = np.where((h > 1e-6) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-6))[0]
        if interior.size < 2:
            break

        order = np.argsort(g[interior])
        low = interior[order[:20]]
        high = interior[order[-20:]]

        cand = np.unique(np.concatenate((low, high)))
        Lcache = {}
        for pp in cand:
            p = int(pp)
            Lcache[p] = deriv_lag_vector(h, n, p)

        raw = []
        for ii in low:
            for jj in high:
                i = int(ii)
                j = int(jj)
                if i != j:
                    raw.append((i, j))

        rcount = min(30, max(1, int(interior.size)))
        for _ in range(rcount):
            a = int(interior[np.random.randint(int(interior.size))])
            b = int(interior[np.random.randint(int(interior.size))])
            if a != b:
                raw.append((a, b))

        scored = []
        for i, j in raw:
            if i == j:
                continue
            scored.append((-abs(float(g[i]) - float(g[j])), i, j))

        scored.sort()
        if len(scored) > int(max_pairs):
            scored = scored[:int(max_pairs)]

        improved = False

        for _, i, j in scored:
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break

            Li = Lcache.get(int(i))
            Lj = Lcache.get(int(j))
            if Li is None or Lj is None:
                continue

            t, bc = pair_line_search(c, h, n, i, j, Li, Lj)

            if bc < maxv - 1e-13:
                old_h = h.copy()
                old_c = c.copy()

                h[int(i)] += t
                h[int(j)] -= t
                h = project_feasible(h, n)
                c_new = compute_c5_vec(h, n)
                newc = float(np.max(c_new))

                if newc < best_c - 1e-14:
                    best_c = newc
                    best_h = h.copy()
                    c = c_new
                    improved = True
                    break
                else:
                    h = old_h
                    c = old_c

        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 8:
                break

            for _ in range(10):
                if time.time() > deadline:
                    break
                a = int(interior[np.random.randint(int(interior.size))])
                b = int(interior[np.random.randint(int(interior.size))])
                if a == b:
                    continue

                if a not in Lcache:
                    Lcache[a] = deriv_lag_vector(h, n, a)
                if b not in Lcache:
                    Lcache[b] = deriv_lag_vector(h, n, b)

                t, bc = pair_line_search(c, h, n, a, b, Lcache[a], Lcache[b])

                if bc < best_c - 1e-13:
                    old_h = h.copy()
                    h[a] += t
                    h[b] -= t
                    h = project_feasible(h, n)
                    c_new = compute_c5_vec(h, n)
                    newc = float(np.max(c_new))

                    if newc < best_c - 1e-14:
                        best_c = newc
                        best_h = h.copy()
                        c = c_new
                        improved = True
                        break
                    else:
                        h = old_h

            if improved:
                no_improve = 0

    return best_h, float(best_c)


def de_lowdim(n, deadline, candidates, max_evals=10000, seed=1):
    np.random.seed(int(seed))

    pop = []
    vals = []

    for cand in candidates:
        h = project_feasible(cand, n)
        pop.append(h)
        vals.append(compute_c5(h, n))

    popsize = max(16, min(60, 4 * int(n)))
    while len(pop) < popsize:
        h = rand_feasible(n)
        pop.append(h)
        vals.append(compute_c5(h, n))

    best_idx = int(np.argmin(vals))
    best_h = pop[best_idx].copy()
    best_c = float(vals[best_idx])

    F = 0.85
    CR = 0.7
    evals = 0
    stagn = 0

    while time.time() < deadline and evals < int(max_evals):
        improved_gen = False
        P = len(pop)

        for i in range(P):
            if time.time() > deadline - 0.1 or evals >= int(max_evals):
                break

            others = [x for x in range(P) if x != i]
            if len(others) < 3:
                break

            chosen = np.random.choice(others, size=3, replace=False)
            r1 = int(chosen[0])
            r2 = int(chosen[1])
            r3 = int(chosen[2])

            mut = pop[r1] + F * (pop[r2] - pop[r3])
            child = pop[i].copy()
            mask = np.random.rand(n) < CR
            if not mask.any():
                mask[int(np.random.randint(n))] = True

            child[mask] = mut[mask]
            child = np.clip(child, 0.0, 1.0)
            child = project_feasible(child, n)

            c = compute_c5(child, n)
            evals += 1

            if c < vals[i]:
                pop[i] = child
                vals[i] = c
                improved_gen = True

            if c < best_c:
                best_c = c
                best_h = child.copy()
                improved_gen = True

        if improved_gen:
            stagn = 0
        else:
            stagn += 1
            if stagn > 5:
                worst = int(np.argmax(vals))
                hh = best_h.copy()
                hh = np.clip(hh + 0.05 * np.random.randn(n), 0.0, 1.0)
                hh = project_feasible(hh, n)
                cc = compute_c5(hh, n)
                evals += 1
                pop[worst] = hh
                vals[worst] = cc
                if cc < best_c:
                    best_c = cc
                    best_h = hh.copy()
                stagn = 0

    order = np.argsort(vals)
    for kk in range(min(3, len(order))):
        idx = int(order[kk])
        hh = pop[idx].copy()
        if time.time() > deadline - 1.0:
            break
        hh, cc = pair_descent(hh, n, deadline, max_iter=10, max_pairs=60)
        if cc < best_c:
            best_c = cc
            best_h = hh.copy()

    return best_h, float(best_c)


def binary_hill_climb(h, n, deadline, max_evals=50000):
    h = make_binary(h, n).astype(float)
    cur = h.copy()
    cur_c = compute_c5(cur, n)
    best = cur.copy()
    best_c = cur_c

    passes = 0
    evals = 0

    while time.time() < deadline and passes < 50 and evals < int(max_evals):
        improved = False

        zeros = np.where(cur < 0.5)[0].tolist()
        ones = np.where(cur > 0.5)[0].tolist()

        if len(zeros) == 0 or len(ones) == 0:
            break

        if int(n) * int(n) > 8000:
            pairs = []
            for _ in range(2000):
                za = int(np.random.randint(len(zeros)))
                oa = int(np.random.randint(len(ones)))
                pairs.append((za, oa))
        else:
            pairs = []
            for za in range(len(zeros)):
                for oa in range(len(ones)):
                    pairs.append((za, oa))

        for za, oa in pairs:
            if evals >= int(max_evals) or time.time() > deadline:
                break

            z = zeros[za]
            o = ones[oa]
            if z == o:
                continue

            oldz = cur[z]
            oldo = cur[o]

            cur[z] = 1.0
            cur[o] = 0.0

            c = compute_c5(cur, n)
            evals += 1

            if c < cur_c - 1e-15:
                cur_c = c
                improved = True
                if c < best_c:
                    best_c = c
                    best = cur.copy()
                break
            else:
                cur[z] = oldz
                cur[o] = oldo

        passes += 1

        if not improved:
            kick_ok = False
            for _ in range(100):
                if evals >= int(max_evals) or time.time() > deadline:
                    break

                z = zeros[int(np.random.randint(len(zeros)))]
                o = ones[int(np.random.randint(len(ones)))]
                if z == o:
                    continue

                oldz = cur[z]
                oldo = cur[o]

                cur[z] = 1.0
                cur[o] = 0.0

                c = compute_c5(cur, n)
                evals += 1

                if c <= cur_c + 1e-12:
                    cur_c = c
                    improved = True
                    kick_ok = True
                    if c < best_c:
                        best_c = c
                        best = cur.copy()
                    break
                else:
                    cur[z] = oldz
                    cur[o] = oldo

            if not kick_ok:
                break

    return best, float(best_c)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 5.0
    np.random.seed(int(seed))

    g = globals()
    if 'initial_h_values' in g and g['initial_h_values'] is not None:
        h_parent = np.asarray(g['initial_h_values'], dtype=float).copy()
    else:
        h_parent = default_h(500)

    n_parent = int(len(h_parent))
    if n_parent < 2:
        h_parent = default_h(500)
        n_parent = int(len(h_parent))

    h_parent = project_feasible(h_parent, n_parent)

    best_h = h_parent.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n_parent)
    best_n = int(n_parent)

    h_parent_opt = h_parent.copy()
    c_parent_opt = compute_c5(h_parent_opt, n_parent)

    sweep_end = min(t0 + 45.0, deadline - 400.0)
    if sweep_end < time.time() + 5.0:
        sweep_end = time.time() + 10.0
    if sweep_end > deadline - 50.0:
        sweep_end = deadline - 50.0

    sweep_n = [8, 10, 12, 14, 16, 20, 24, 28, 32, 40, 48, 64, 80, 96, 128, 160, 200, 256, 320, 400, 500, 640, 800, 1000]
    for m in sweep_n:
        if time.time() > sweep_end:
            break
        if m > 2000:
            continue
        try:
            hn = resample_by_mass(h_parent, m)
            c = compute_c5(hn, m)
            if np.isfinite(c) and c < best_c:
                best_c = c
                best_h = hn
                best_n = int(m)
        except Exception:
            pass

    low_end = min(t0 + 380.0, deadline - 300.0)
    if low_end < time.time() + 20.0:
        low_end = time.time() + 20.0
    if low_end > deadline - 60.0:
        low_end = deadline - 60.0

    low_list = [8, 10, 12, 14, 16, 20, 24, 28, 32, 40, 48, 64, 80, 96, 128]

    for pos, n in enumerate(low_list):
        if time.time() >= low_end:
            break

        remaining_counts = max(1, len(low_list) - pos)
        alloc = max(10.0, (low_end - time.time()) / float(remaining_counts))
        alloc = min(45.0, alloc)

        if time.time() + 10.0 > low_end:
            break

        cands = []
        try:
            cands.append(resample_by_mass(h_parent, n))
        except Exception:
            pass
        cands.append(rand_feasible(n))
        if int(n) % 2 == 0:
            cands.append(rand_binary(n))

        init_h = cands[0]
        init_c = 1e9
        for cand in cands:
            cand = project_feasible(cand, n)
            cc = compute_c5(cand, n)
            if cc < init_c:
                init_h = cand
                init_c = cc

        hb = init_h
        cb = init_c

        sub_end = time.time() + alloc * 0.55
        if sub_end > deadline:
            sub_end = deadline

        if n <= 64 and sub_end > time.time() + 5.0:
            if n <= 12:
                max_ev = 40000
            elif n <= 16:
                max_ev = 25000
            elif n <= 24:
                max_ev = 15000
            elif n <= 32:
                max_ev = 10000
            elif n <= 48:
                max_ev = 6000
            else:
                max_ev = 3500
            try:
                hb, cb = de_lowdim(n, sub_end, cands, max_evals=max_ev, seed=int(seed) + int(n))
            except Exception:
                hb, cb = init_h, init_c

        elif sub_end > time.time() + 5.0:
            try:
                hb, cb = lbfgs_optimize(init_h, n, sub_end, maxiter_override=300)
            except Exception:
                hb, cb = init_h, init_c

        if np.isfinite(cb) and cb < best_c:
            best_c = cb
            best_h = hb.copy()
            best_n = int(n)

        if (int(n) % 2 == 0) and n <= 128:
            bin_end = time.time() + min(alloc * 0.35, 30.0)
            for start in range(2):
                if bin_end <= time.time() + 2.0:
                    break
                try:
                    if start == 0:
                        binit = make_binary(resample_by_mass(h_parent, n), n)
                    else:
                        binit = rand_binary(n)

                    if n <= 32:
                        max_ev = 30000
                    elif n <= 64:
                        max_ev = 15000
                    else:
                        max_ev = 8000

                    hb2, cb2 = binary_hill_climb(binit, n, bin_end, max_evals=max_ev)

                    if np.isfinite(cb2) and cb2 < best_c:
                        best_c = cb2
                        best_h = hb2.copy()
                        best_n = int(n)

                    if cb2 <= best_c + 0.005 and time.time() < low_end - 2.0:
                        hb3, cb3 = pair_descent(hb2, n, min(time.time() + 5.0, low_end), max_iter=8, max_pairs=60)
                        if np.isfinite(cb3) and cb3 < best_c:
                            best_c = cb3
                            best_h = hb3.copy()
                            best_n = int(n)
                except Exception:
                    pass

        if n <= 200 and time.time() < low_end - 5.0:
            local_end = time.time() + min(alloc * 0.3, 25.0)
            try:
                hloc = hb.copy()
                cl = compute_c5(hloc, n)
                if n <= 128:
                    hloc, cl = pair_descent(hloc, n, local_end, max_iter=30, max_pairs=150)
                if time.time() < local_end - 3.0:
                    hloc, cl = sqp_optimize(hloc, n, local_end, max_iter=40, max_active=80)
                if np.isfinite(cl) and cl < best_c:
                    best_c = cl
                    best_h = hloc.copy()
                    best_n = int(n)
            except Exception:
                pass

    med_end = deadline - 80.0
    if time.time() < med_end - 30.0 and n_parent <= 1200:
        cand = h_parent.copy()
        cn = int(n_parent)
        cc = compute_c5(cand, cn)
        avail = med_end - time.time()

        try:
            tsqp = time.time() + min(70.0, avail * 0.4)
            if cn <= 1000:
                max_it_sqp = 120 if cn <= 500 else 70
                cand, cc = sqp_optimize(cand, cn, tsqp, max_iter=max_it_sqp)
            if np.isfinite(cc) and cc < c_parent_opt:
                c_parent_opt = cc
                h_parent_opt = cand.copy()
            if np.isfinite(cc) and cc < best_c:
                best_c = cc
                best_h = cand.copy()
                best_n = cn
        except Exception:
            pass

        if time.time() < med_end - 25.0:
            try:
                tlbf = time.time() + min(70.0, (med_end - time.time()) * 0.4)
                if cn <= 600:
                    cand, cc = lbfgs_optimize(cand, cn, tlbf, maxiter_override=600 if cn <= 200 else 400)
                else:
                    cand, cc = lbfgs_optimize(cand, cn, tlbf, maxiter_override=250)
                if np.isfinite(cc) and cc < c_parent_opt:
                    c_parent_opt = cc
                    h_parent_opt = cand.copy()
                if np.isfinite(cc) and cc < best_c:
                    best_c = cc
                    best_h = cand.copy()
                    best_n = cn
            except Exception:
                pass

        if time.time() < med_end - 10.0 and cn <= 800:
            try:
                cand, cc = pair_descent(cand, cn, med_end - 10.0, max_iter=30, max_pairs=120)
                if np.isfinite(cc) and cc < c_parent_opt:
                    c_parent_opt = cc
                    h_parent_opt = cand.copy()
                if np.isfinite(cc) and cc < best_c:
                    best_c = cc
                    best_h = cand.copy()
                    best_n = cn
            except Exception:
                pass

    if time.time() < deadline - 30.0 and best_n != n_parent and best_n <= 1500:
        cand = best_h.copy()
        n = int(best_n)
        cc = best_c
        avail = max(0.0, (deadline - 30.0) - time.time())

        try:
            if n <= 128:
                if time.time() < deadline - 15.0:
                    cand, cc = pair_descent(cand, n, time.time() + min(30.0, avail * 0.4), max_iter=60, max_pairs=200)
                if time.time() < deadline - 10.0:
                    ma = 200 if n <= 64 else 120
                    cand, cc = sqp_optimize(cand, n, time.time() + min(20.0, avail * 0.3), max_iter=80, max_active=ma)
                if time.time() < deadline - 5.0:
                    cand, cc = lbfgs_optimize(cand, n, time.time() + min(20.0, max(0.0, deadline - time.time()) * 0.3), maxiter_override=400)
                if n <= 80 and time.time() < deadline - 5.0:
                    cand, cc = slqp_polish(cand, n, deadline - 3.0, alpha=50000.0, maxiter=300)

            elif n <= 600:
                if time.time() < deadline - 30.0:
                    cand, cc = sqp_optimize(cand, n, time.time() + min(40.0, avail * 0.4), max_iter=80, max_active=80)
                if time.time() < deadline - 15.0:
                    cand, cc = lbfgs_optimize(cand, n, time.time() + min(30.0, avail * 0.3), maxiter_override=300)
                if time.time() < deadline - 5.0:
                    cand, cc = pair_descent(cand, n, deadline - 5.0, max_iter=25, max_pairs=120)

            else:
                if time.time() < deadline - 20.0:
                    cand, cc = sqp_optimize(cand, n, deadline - 15.0, max_iter=40, max_active=40)

            if np.isfinite(cc) and cc < best_c:
                best_c = cc
                best_h = cand.copy()
                best_n = n
        except Exception:
            pass

    sweep2_end = min(deadline - 10.0, time.time() + 25.0)
    small_ns = [8, 10, 12, 14, 16, 20, 24, 32, 40, 48, 64, 80, 96, 128, 160, 200, 256, 320, 400]

    sources = [best_h, h_parent_opt]
    for src in sources:
        if time.time() > sweep2_end:
            break
        for m in small_ns:
            if time.time() > sweep2_end:
                break
            try:
                hn = resample_by_mass(src, m)
                cc = compute_c5(hn, m)
                if np.isfinite(cc) and cc < best_c:
                    best_c = cc
                    best_h = hn
                    best_n = int(m)
            except Exception:
                pass

    if time.time() < deadline - 8.0:
        n = int(best_n)
        cand = best_h.copy()
        try:
            if n <= 200:
                cand, cc = slqp_polish(cand, n, deadline - 3.0, alpha=100000.0, maxiter=300)
            elif n <= 600:
                cand, cc = pair_descent(cand, n, deadline - 3.0, max_iter=15, max_pairs=80)
            elif n <= 1200:
                cand, cc = sqp_optimize(cand, n, deadline - 3.0, max_iter=10, max_active=20)
            else:
                cc = compute_c5(cand, n)

            if np.isfinite(cc) and cc < best_c:
                best_c = cc
                best_h = cand.copy()
                best_n = n
        except Exception:
            pass

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, int(best_n))
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, int(best_n))

    if not np.isfinite(best_c):
        best_h = project_feasible(h_parent, int(n_parent))
        best_c = compute_c5(best_h, int(n_parent))
        best_n = int(n_parent)

    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```
